    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिघृक्ष्</span> <i>jighṛkṣ</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-grah" class="skt-link">grah</a>] v. pr. <i>(jighṛkṣati)</i> pr. md. <i>(jighṛkṣate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>jighṛkṣita</i><i>)</i> vouloir s'emparer de, vouloir saisir, avoir l'intention de prendre, convoiter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jigh_rk_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिघृक्षा</span> <i>jighṛkṣā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-jigh_rk_s" class="skt-link">jighṛkṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-jigh_rk_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> intention de saisir, convoitise.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jigh_rk_sita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिघृक्षित</span> <i>jighṛkṣita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-jigh_rk_s" class="skt-link">jighṛkṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jigh_rk_sita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jigh_rk_sita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jigh_rk_sitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jighṛkṣitā</i> convoité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jigh_rk_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिघृक्षु</span> <i>jighṛkṣu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jigh_rk_s" class="skt-link">jighṛkṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jigh_rk_su" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jigh_rk_su" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jigh_rk_su" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui veut prendre, qui veut voler.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jighaa_ms">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिघांस्</span> <i>jighāṃs</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-ghas" class="skt-link">ghas</a>] v. pr. <i>(jighāṃsati)</i> pr. md. <i>(jighāṃsate)</i> vouloir tuer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jighaa_msaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिघांसा</span> <i>jighāṃsā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-jighaa_ms" class="skt-link">jighāṃs</a>] <a href="#entry-jighaa_msaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> désir de meurtre ou de destruction; vengeance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jighaa_msin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिघांसिन्</span> <i>jighāṃsin</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jighaa_ms" class="skt-link">jighāṃs</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jighaa_msin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jighaa_msin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jighaa_msinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jighāṃsinī</i> qui désire tuer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jighaa_msu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिघांसु</span> <i>jighāṃsu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jighaa_ms" class="skt-link">jighāṃs</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jighaa_msu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jighaa_msu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jighaa_msu" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui désire tuer — <a href="#entry-jighaa_msu" class="skt-link">m.</a> ennemi.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jighats">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिघत्स्</span> <i>jighats</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-ghas" class="skt-link">ghas</a>] v. pr. <i>(jighatsati)</i> vouloir dévorer; consumer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jighatsu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिघत्सु</span> <i>jighatsu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jighats" class="skt-link">jighats</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jighatsu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jighatsu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jighatsu" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui veut dévorer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jighra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिघ्र</span> <i>jighra</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ghraa" class="skt-link">ghrā</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-jighra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jighra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jighraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jighrā</i> qui flaire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui observe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jigii_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिगीष्</span> <i>jigīṣ</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-ji" class="skt-link">ji</a>] v. pr. md. <i>(jigīṣate)</i> vouloir vaincre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jigii_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिगीषा</span> <i>jigīṣā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-jigii_s" class="skt-link">jigīṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-jigii_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> désir de vaincre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jigii_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिगीषु</span> <i>jigīṣu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jigii_s" class="skt-link">jigīṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jigii_su" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jigii_su" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jigii_su" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui désire la victoire <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> ambitieux.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jihiir_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिहीर्ष्</span> <i>jihīrṣ</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-h_r_1" class="skt-link">hṛ_1</a>] v. pr. <i>(jihīrṣati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>jihīrṣita</i><i>)</i> vouloir enlever.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jihiir_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिहीर्षा</span> <i>jihīrṣā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-jihiir_s" class="skt-link">jihīrṣ</a>] <a href="#entry-jihiir_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> désir, intention de saisir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jihiir_su">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिहीर्षु</span> <i>jihīrṣu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jihiir_s" class="skt-link">jihīrṣ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jihiir_su" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jihiir_su" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jihiir_su" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui cherche à prendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jihma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिह्म</span> <i>jihma</i> a. <a href="#entry-jihma" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jihma" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jihmaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jihmā</i> oblique, transverse; en travers <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui cligne (œil) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> tortueux, retors; trompeur, malhonnête <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lent, paresseux; faible, émoussé — <a href="#entry-jihma" class="skt-link">n.</a> mensonge, malhonnêteté — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. <i>(jihmāyati)</i> se tromper de chemin — v. [<span class="skt-grammar">11</span>] pr. md. <i>(jihmāyate)</i> être oblique; hésiter à ⟨inf.⟩.<br> <i>jihmam</i> <i>i</i> aller de travers; prendre le mauvais chemin; rater le but ⟨abl.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jihmopaaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jihmopāya</i> [<a href="#entry-upaaya" class="skt-link">upāya</a>] <a href="#entry-jihmopaaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> stratagème retors; traîtrise.<br> <a href="#entry-jihma" class="skt-link">jihma</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jihvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिह्वा</span> <i>jihvā</i> <a href="#entry-jihvaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <a href="#entry-jihva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>jihva</i> langue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-saafkhya" class="skt-link">sāṃkhya</a>] le sens [<a href="#entry-buddhiindriya" class="skt-link">buddhīndriya</a>] du goût; son régent [<a href="#entry-niyant_r" class="skt-link">niyantṛ</a>] est <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a>; la perception [<a href="#entry-tanmaatra" class="skt-link">tanmātra</a>] associée est la saveur [<a href="#entry-rasa" class="skt-link">rasa</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> langue de feu; flamme; cf. <a href="#entry-saptajihva" class="skt-link">saptajihva</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>lingua</i>; ang. language, tongue; fr. langue.<br> <i>dvijihva</i> m. [«qui a la langue fourchue»] serpent; fourbe.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jihvaabandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jihvābandha</i> [<a href="#entry-bandha" class="skt-link">bandha</a>] <a href="#entry-jihvaabandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] position de la langue retournée et étayée contre la voûte du palais.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jihvaagra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jihvāgra</i> [<a href="#entry-agra" class="skt-link">agra</a>] <a href="#entry-jihvaagra" class="skt-link">n.</a> bout de la langue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jihvaamuula">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jihvāmūla</i> [<a href="#entry-muula" class="skt-link">mūla</a>] <a href="#entry-jihvaamuula" class="skt-link">n.</a> base de la langue (point d'articulation des gutturales).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jihvaamuuliiya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jihvāmūlīya</i> [<a href="#entry--iiya" class="skt-link">-īya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jihvaamuuliiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jihvaamuuliiya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jihvaamuuliiyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jihvāmūlīyā</i> phon. se dit des gutturales, not. du <a href="#entry-visarga" class="skt-link">visarga</a> guttural; opp. <a href="#entry-upadhmaaniiya" class="skt-link">upadhmānīya</a> — <a href="#entry-jihvaamuuliiya" class="skt-link">m.</a> phon. (véd.) fricative vélaire sourde, représentée par écrit par le <a href="#entry-ardhavisarga" class="skt-link">ardhavisarga</a>, remplacée en sanskrit classique par le <a href="#entry-visarga" class="skt-link">visarga</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-jihvaamuula" class="skt-link">jihvāmūla</a> <br> <a href="#entry-jihvaa" class="skt-link">jihvā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jihvaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jihvānta</i> [<a href="#entry-anta" class="skt-link">anta</a>] <a href="#entry-jihvaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> pl. <i>jihvāntau</i> extrémités de la langue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jihvaapa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jihvāpa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_1" class="skt-link">pa_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jihvaapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jihvaapa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jihvaapaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jihvāpā</i> se dit d'un animal qui lappe — <a href="#entry-jihvaapa" class="skt-link">m.</a> chien, ours, félin, etc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जी</span> <i>jī</i> [<a href="#entry-jiiva" class="skt-link">jīva</a>] ifc. <a href="#entry-jii" class="skt-link">m.</a> (adresse, après nom) Monsieur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiimuuta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जीमूत</span> <i>jīmūta</i> <a href="#entry-jiimuuta" class="skt-link">m.</a> nuage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> np. de cheval <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. [<a href="#entry-Umahaabhaarata" class="skt-link">Mah.</a>] np. de Jīmūta, lutteur invincible venu à la cour du roi <a href="#entry-Uviraa_ta" class="skt-link">Virāṭa</a> défier <a href="#entry-Ubhiima" class="skt-link">Bhīma</a>-<a href="#entry-Uballava" class="skt-link">Ballava</a>, qui l'écrasa.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiimuutaketu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīmūtaketu</i> [<a href="#entry-ketu" class="skt-link">ketu</a>] <a href="#entry-jiimuutaketu" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. [<a href="#entry-Uhitopadeza" class="skt-link">Hitopadeśa</a>] np. de Jīmūtaketu «Qui a un nuage pour bannière», roi de Ceylan; le <a href="#entry-vidyaadhara" class="skt-link">vidyādhara</a> <a href="#entry-Ukandarpaketu" class="skt-link">Kandarpaketu</a> est son fils.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiimuutavaahana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīmūtavāhana</i> [<a href="#entry-vaahana" class="skt-link">vāhana</a>] <a href="#entry-jiimuutavaahana" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. du prince <a href="#entry-vidyaadhara" class="skt-link">vidyādhara</a> Jīmūtavāhana «Porté par un nuage» qui, jeune marié, demanda à <a href="#entry-Ugaru_da" class="skt-link">Garuḍa</a> de le dévorer vivant pour sauver la vie d'un serpent; cf. <a href="#entry-Unaagaananda" class="skt-link">Nāgānanda</a>, <a href="#entry-Uzilaahaara" class="skt-link">Śilāhāra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. d'<a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> «Chevauchant les nuées».<br> <a href="#entry-jiimuuta" class="skt-link">jīmūta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiir_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जीर्ण</span> <i>jīrṇa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-j_rr" class="skt-link">jṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jiir_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jiir_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jiir_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jīrṇā</i> usé, vieux, vieilli, décrépit, délabré <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> dissous, digéré, avalé.<br> <i>jīrṇam</i> <i>aṅge</i> <i>subhāṣitam</i> Les bonnes paroles me sont restées dans la gorge.<br> <i>bhogā</i> <i>na</i> <i>bhuktā</i> <i>vayam</i> <i>eva</i> <i>bhuktās</i> <i>tapo</i> <i>na</i> <i>taptaṃ</i> <i>vayam</i> <i>eva</i> <i>taptāḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> <i>kālo</i> <i>na</i> <i>yāto</i> <i>vayam</i> <i>eva</i> <i>yātās</i> <i>tṛṣṇā</i> <i>na</i> <i>jīrṇā</i> <i>vayam</i> <i>eva</i> <i>jīrṇāḥ</i> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> De plaisirs non rassasiés nous voilà lassés; de mortifications sans fin nous voilà brûlés; de bon temps jamais terminé nous voilà épuisés; de désirs jamais assouvis nous voilà usés.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiir_navastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīrṇavastra</i> [<a href="#entry-vastra" class="skt-link">vastra</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jiir_navastra" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jiir_navastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jiir_navastraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jīrṇavastrā</i> déguenillé.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiir_noddhaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīrṇoddhāra</i> [<a href="#entry-uddhaara" class="skt-link">uddhāra</a>] <a href="#entry-jiir_noddhaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> restauration, réparation, rénovation.<br> <a href="#entry-jiir_na" class="skt-link">jīrṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiira_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīraṇa</i> var. <i>jaraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-jiira_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jiira_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> graine de cumin.<br> <a href="#entry-jiira_2" class="skt-link">jīra_2</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiira_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जीर</span> <i>jīra_1</i> [<a href="#entry-jinv" class="skt-link">jinv</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jiira_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jiira_1" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jiiraa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jīrā</i> vif, rapide <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui conduit ⟨g.⟩ — <a href="#entry-jiira_1" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. mouvement rapide (de la meule de <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> épée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiira_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जीर</span> <i>jīra_2</i> <a href="#entry-jiira_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. bot. <i>Cuminum cyminum</i>, cumin.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiiradaanu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīradānu</i> [<a href="#entry-daanu" class="skt-link">dānu</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jiiradaanu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jiiradaanu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jiiradaanu" class="skt-link">f.</a> au cours vif.<br> <a href="#entry-jiira_1" class="skt-link">jīra_1</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiiraka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīraka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-jiiraka" class="skt-link">m.</a> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiiv">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">जीव्</span> <i>jīv</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(jīvati)</i> pr. md. <i>(jīvate)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>jīviṣyati</i><i>)</i> pft. <i>(jijīva)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>jīvita</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>jīvitvā</i>, <i>-jīvya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>ut</i>, <i>upa</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> vivre, être vivant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> vivre de, se nourrir de ⟨i.⟩ — dés. <i>(jijīviṣati)</i> désirer vivre <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gr. βιος; lat. <i>vivo</i>; fr. vivre, biologie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जीव</span> <i>jīva</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jiiv" class="skt-link">jīv</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jiiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jiiva" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jīvā</i> vivant, vif — <a href="#entry-jiiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> vie; existence; âme individuelle <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. np. de Jīva, le Microcosme ou l'Homme <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. de <a href="#entry-Uzriila" class="skt-link">Śrīla</a> <a href="#entry-Ujiiva" class="skt-link">Jīva</a> <a href="#entry-Ugosvaamii" class="skt-link">Gosvāmī</a>, disciple de <a href="#entry-Ucaitanya" class="skt-link">Caitanya</a> (1555–1600); c'est l'un des six pasteurs du <a href="#entry-Uv_rndaavana" class="skt-link">Vṛndāvana</a> [<a href="#entry-gosvaaminas" class="skt-link">gosvāminas</a>]; il composa le <a href="#entry-Uharinaamaam_rtavyaakara_na" class="skt-link">Harināmāmṛtavyākaraṇa</a>, grammaire du sanskrit dévotionnel d'obédience <a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a>; il est aussi l'auteur du <a href="#entry-Uparamaatmasandarbha" class="skt-link">Paramātmasandarbha</a> — <a href="#entry-jiivaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jīvā</i> vie <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> la Terre, le vivant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. corde ou arc de cercle; syn. <a href="#entry-jyaa_3" class="skt-link">jyā_3</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>vivus</i>; fr. vivant.<br> <i>jīvam</i> adv. pendant toute la vie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivaanta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīvānta</i> [<a href="#entry-anta" class="skt-link">anta</a>] <a href="#entry-jiivaanta" class="skt-link">m.</a> mort.<br> <a href="#entry-jiiva" class="skt-link">jīva</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivaatman">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīvātman</i> [<a href="#entry-aatman" class="skt-link">ātman</a>] <a href="#entry-jiivaatman" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. l'Âme individuelle; l'Être incarné.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जीवक</span> <i>jīvaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jiiv" class="skt-link">jīv</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jiivaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jīvikā</i> vivant — <a href="#entry-jiivaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> être vivant — <a href="#entry-jiivikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jīvikā</i> mode de vie — ifc. subsistance par.<br> <i>jīvakaḥ</i> (bénédiction) longue vie à lui.<br> <i>yasyāgamaḥ</i> <i>kevalajīvikāyai</i> <i>taṃ</i> <i>jñānapaṇyaṃ</i> <i>vaṇijaṃ</i> <i>vadanti</i> [<a href="#entry-Ukaalidaasa" class="skt-link">Kālidāsa</a>] Celui dont le seul moyen de subsistance est d'enseigner les écritures, on dit de lui qu'il est un marchand de savoir à vendre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivam">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जीवम्</span> <i>jīvam</i> [acc. <a href="#entry-jiiva" class="skt-link">jīva</a>] iic.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जीवन</span> <i>jīvana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jiiv" class="skt-link">jīv</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jiivana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivanii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jīvanī</i> vivifiant; qui donne la vie — <a href="#entry-jiivana" class="skt-link">n.</a> manière de vivre; moyen de subsistance <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> eau <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> charbon ardent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivanaz">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīvanaś</i> [<a href="#entry-naz_2" class="skt-link">naś_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jiivanaz" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivanaz" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivanaz" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. se dit d'un sacrifice où l'on tue un être vivant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivanmukta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīvanmukta</i> [<a href="#entry-mukta" class="skt-link">mukta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jiivanmukta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivanmukta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivanmuktaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jīvanmuktā</i> phil. [«délivré vivant»] qui a accompli la libération spirituelle de son vivant (et ainsi peut vaquer aux affaires du monde sans attachement et sans souillure).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivanmukti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīvanmukti</i> [<a href="#entry-mukti" class="skt-link">mukti</a>] <a href="#entry-jiivanmukti" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. [«délivrance en cette vie»] état de libération, but ultime du <a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivanmuktiviveka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīvanmuktiviveka</i> [<a href="#entry-viveka" class="skt-link">viveka</a>] <a href="#entry-jiivanmuktiviveka" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Jīvanmuktiviveka, œuvre d'<a href="#entry-advaita" class="skt-link">advaita</a> <a href="#entry-vedaanta" class="skt-link">vedānta</a> due à <a href="#entry-Umaadhavaacaarya" class="skt-link">Mādhavācārya</a>, en défense de <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṃkara_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-jiivanmukti" class="skt-link">jīvanmukti</a> <br> <a href="#entry-jiivat" class="skt-link">jīvat</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जीवत्</span> <i>jīvat</i> [ppr. <a href="#entry-jiiv" class="skt-link">jīv</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jiivat" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivat" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivaantii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jīvāntī</i> vivant.<br> <i>mā</i> <i>jīvan</i> puisse-t-il ne pas vivre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiiva_njiiva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīvaṃjīva</i> [<a href="#entry-jiiva" class="skt-link">jīva</a>] <a href="#entry-jiiva_mjiiva" class="skt-link">m.</a> natu. perdrix.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiiva_njiivaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīvaṃjīvaka</i> [<a href="#entry--ka" class="skt-link">-ka</a>] <a href="#entry-jiiva_mjiivaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> id.<br> <a href="#entry-jiiva_njiiva" class="skt-link">jīvaṃjīva</a> <br> <a href="#entry-jiivam" class="skt-link">jīvam</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जीविका</span> <i>jīvikā</i> iic. f. de <a href="#entry-jiivaka" class="skt-link">jīvaka</a>.<br> <i>jīvikā</i> <i>kṛ</i> gagner sa vie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivikaapraapta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīvikāprāpta</i> [<a href="#entry-praapta" class="skt-link">prāpta</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jiivikaapraapta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivikaapraapta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivikaapraaptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jīvikāprāptā</i> qui a obtenu de quoi vivre.<br> <a href="#entry-jiivikaa" class="skt-link">jīvikā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जीविन्</span> <i>jīvin</i> [<a href="#entry-jiiva" class="skt-link">jīva</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jiivin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivinii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jīvinī</i> qui vit; qui vit de ou par.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जीवित</span> <i>jīvita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-jiiv" class="skt-link">jīv</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jiivita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jiivitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jīvitā</i> vivant — <a href="#entry-jiivita" class="skt-link">n.</a> vie; vitalité <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>vita</i>; fr. vie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jiivitaa_azaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jīvitāaśā</i> [<a href="#entry-aazaa_2" class="skt-link">āśā_2</a>] <a href="#entry-jiivitaa_azaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> espoir de vivre.<br> <i>jīvitāaśā</i> <i>dustyajā</i> Il est difficle de renoncer à l'espoir de vivre.<br> <a href="#entry-jiivita" class="skt-link">jīvita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jij_naas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिज्ञास्</span> <i>jijñās</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-j_naa_1" class="skt-link">jñā_1</a>] v. pr. md. <i>(jijñāsate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>jijñāsita</i><i>)</i> pfp. <i>(</i><i>jijñāsitavya</i><i>)</i> désirer savoir.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jij_naasaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिज्ञासा</span> <i>jijñāsā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-jij_naas" class="skt-link">jijñās</a>] <a href="#entry-jij_naasaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> désir de savoir, curiosité; enquête, investigation <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. intention du locuteur — <a href="#entry-jij_naasa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <i>jijñāsa</i> ifc. qui investigue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jij_naasaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिज्ञासक</span> <i>jijñāsaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jij_naas" class="skt-link">jijñās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jij_naasaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jij_naasaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jij_naasikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jijñāsikā</i> cf. <a href="#entry-jij_naasu" class="skt-link">jijñāsu</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jij_naasana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिज्ञासन</span> <i>jijñāsana</i> [act. <a href="#entry-jij_naas" class="skt-link">jijñās</a>] <a href="#entry-jij_naasana" class="skt-link">n.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-jij_naasaa" class="skt-link">jijñāsā</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jij_naasita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिज्ञासित</span> <i>jijñāsita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-jij_naas" class="skt-link">jijñās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jij_naasita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jij_naasita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jij_naasitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jijñāsitā</i> investigué; enquêté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jij_naasitavya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिज्ञासितव्य</span> <i>jijñāsitavya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <a href="#entry-jij_naas" class="skt-link">jijñās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jij_naasitavya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jij_naasitavya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jij_naasitavyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jijñāsitavyā</i> à investiguer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jij_naasu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिज्ञासु</span> <i>jijñāsu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jij_naas" class="skt-link">jijñās</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jij_naasu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jij_naasu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jij_naasu" class="skt-link">f.</a> curieux, investigateur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jina">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिन</span> <i>jina</i> [<a href="#entry-ji" class="skt-link">ji</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-jina" class="skt-link">m.</a> vainqueur, conquérant <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. saint <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a>; not. np. de Jina «le Vainqueur», épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvardhamaana" class="skt-link">Vardhamāna</a>-<a href="#entry-Umahaaviira" class="skt-link">Mahāvīra</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubuddha" class="skt-link">Buddha</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> bd. [<a href="#entry-Umahaayaana" class="skt-link">Mahāyāna</a>] être spirituel accompli; not. gardien de paradis (<a href="#entry-Uak_sobhya" class="skt-link">Akṣobhya</a>, <a href="#entry-Uamitaabha" class="skt-link">Amitābha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uamoghasiddhi" class="skt-link">Amoghasiddhi</a>, <a href="#entry-Uratnasambhava" class="skt-link">Ratnasaṃbhava</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvairocana" class="skt-link">Vairocana</a>) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. épith. de <a href="#entry-Uvi_s_nu" class="skt-link">Viṣṇu</a> «Vainqueur».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jinaratna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jinaratna</i> [<a href="#entry-ratna" class="skt-link">ratna</a>] <a href="#entry-jinaratna" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du moine jaïne Jinaratna (Rajasthan, 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); il composa le poème Līlāvatīsāra.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jinasena">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jinasena</i> [<a href="#entry-senaa" class="skt-link">senā</a>] <a href="#entry-jinasena" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du maître jaïne <a href="#entry-aacaarya" class="skt-link">ācārya</a> Jinasena, disciple de <a href="#entry-Uviirasena_1" class="skt-link">Vīrasena_1</a> (9⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle); on lui doit le <a href="#entry-Upaarzvaabhyudaya" class="skt-link">Pārśvābhyudaya</a> et le <a href="#entry-Uvardhamanapuraa_na" class="skt-link">Vardhamanapurāṇa</a>; son disciple <a href="#entry-Ugu_nabhadra" class="skt-link">Guṇabhadra</a> compléta son <a href="#entry-Uaadipuraa_na_2" class="skt-link">Ādipurāṇa_2</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jinendra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jinendra</i> [<a href="#entry-indra" class="skt-link">indra</a>] <a href="#entry-jinendra" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. être accompli [<a href="#entry-buddha" class="skt-link">buddha</a>] <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> jn. saint <a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hist. np. du grammarien Jinendra; cf. <a href="#entry-Ujinendrabuddhi" class="skt-link">Jinendrabuddhi</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jinendrabuddhi">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jinendrabuddhi</i> [<a href="#entry-buddhi" class="skt-link">buddhi</a>] <a href="#entry-jinendrabuddhi" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du grammarien Jinendrabuddhi, auteur du <a href="#entry-Unyaasa" class="skt-link">Nyāsa</a>, commentaire de la grammaire de Bénarès [<a href="#entry-Ukaazikaa" class="skt-link">Kāśikā</a>]; il vivait vers 700.<br> <a href="#entry-jinendra" class="skt-link">jinendra</a> <br> <a href="#entry-jina" class="skt-link">jina</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jinv">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिन्व्</span> <i>jinv</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-jiiv" class="skt-link">jīv</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(jinvati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>jinvita</i><i>)</i> inciter, exciter; activer; animer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jinvita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जिन्वित</span> <i>jinvita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-jinv" class="skt-link">jinv</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jinvita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jinvita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jinvitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jinvitā</i> excité.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jit">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जित्</span> <i>jit</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ji" class="skt-link">ji</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-jit" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jit" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jit" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui vainc, vainqueur de <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui vainc par.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जित</span> <i>jita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ji" class="skt-link">ji</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jitā</i> vaincu — iic. qui a vaincu — ifc. vaincu par.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jitakrodha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jitakrodha</i> [<a href="#entry-krodha" class="skt-link">krodha</a>] <a href="#entry-jitakrodha" class="skt-link">m.</a> qui domine sa colère.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jitapraaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jitaprāya</i> [<a href="#entry-praaya" class="skt-link">prāya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jitapraaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jitapraaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jitapraayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jitaprāyā</i> presqu'entièrement vaincu.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jitendriya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jitendriya</i> [<a href="#entry-indriya" class="skt-link">indriya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jitendriya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jitendriya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jitendriyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jitendriyā</i> qui domine ses sens.<br> <a href="#entry-jita" class="skt-link">jita</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jo_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जोष</span> <i>joṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-ju_s_1" class="skt-link">juṣ_1</a>] <a href="#entry-jo_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> plaisir, satisfaction, contentement.<br> <i>joṣam</i> adv. [tranquillement] en silence <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> à son bon plaisir.<br> <i>joṣam</i> <i>ās</i> être satisfait; se calmer; rester silencieux.<br> <i>joṣam</i> <i>sthā</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jo_st_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जोष्तृ</span> <i>joṣtṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ju_s_1" class="skt-link">juṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jo_st_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jo_st_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jo_strii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>joṣtrī</i> qui chérit, qui aime.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jonaraaja">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जोनराज</span> <i>jonarāja</i> <a href="#entry-jonaraaja" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Jonarāja, écrivain du <a href="#entry-Ukazmiira" class="skt-link">Kaśmīra</a> de la fin du 14⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il compléta la <a href="#entry-Uraajatarafgi_nii" class="skt-link">Rājataraṅgiṇī</a> de <a href="#entry-Ukalha_na" class="skt-link">Kalhaṇa</a> avec sa <a href="#entry-Udvitiiyaraajatarafgi_nii" class="skt-link">Dvitīyarājataraṅgiṇī</a> qui couvre les rois hindous de 1148 à 1339, puis la dynastie islamique fondée par Shah Mīr, jusqu'à sa mort en 1459.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ju_s_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जुष्ट</span> <i>juṣṭa</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-ju_s_1" class="skt-link">juṣ_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-ju_s_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ju_s_ta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ju_s_taa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>juṣṭā</i> content, satisfait <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> apprécié, aimé, agréable; bienvenu; fréquenté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ju_s_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">जुष्</span> <i>juṣ_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">6</span>] pr. md. <i>(juṣate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>juṣṭa</i><i>)</i> goûter, apprécier, prendre plaisir à ⟨acc. g.⟩; aimer; s'adonner à ⟨acc.⟩ — ca. md. <i>(joṣayate)</i> chérir, préférer ⟨acc.⟩ — ca. <i>(joṣayati)</i> faire apprécier <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>gusto</i>; all. kosten; gr. γευω; fr. goûter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-ju_s_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जुष्</span> <i>juṣ_2</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-ju_s_1" class="skt-link">juṣ_1</a>] ifc. a. <a href="#entry-ju_s_2" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-ju_s_2" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-ju_s_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui goûte, qui apprécie, qui trouve son plaisir à; dévoué à <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui réside en, qui visite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jugups">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जुगुप्स्</span> <i>jugups</i> [dés. <a href="#entry-gup" class="skt-link">gup</a>] v. pr. md. <i>(jugupsate)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>jugupsita</i><i>)</i> se défendre de ⟨abl.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> être sur ses gardes; se méfier de, éviter, détester, mépriser ⟨acc.⟩.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jugupsaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जुगुप्सा</span> <i>jugupsā</i> [act. <a href="#entry-jugups" class="skt-link">jugups</a>] <a href="#entry-jugupsaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> aversion, dégoût; horreur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. le dégoût, une émotion de base [<a href="#entry-sthaayibhaava" class="skt-link">sthāyibhāva</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jugupsita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जुगुप्सित</span> <i>jugupsita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-jugups" class="skt-link">jugups</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jugupsita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jugupsita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jugupsitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jugupsitā</i> détesté; répugnant — <a href="#entry-jugupsita" class="skt-link">n.</a> action répugnante ou horrible.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jugupsu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जुगुप्सु</span> <i>jugupsu</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jugups" class="skt-link">jugups</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jugupsu" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jugupsu" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jugupsu" class="skt-link">f.</a> qui évite, qui déteste, qui méprise; qui éprouve du dégoût pour.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-juhuu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जुहू</span> <i>juhū</i> [red. <a href="#entry-hu" class="skt-link">hu</a>] <a href="#entry-juhuu" class="skt-link">f.</a> langue <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> cuiller à oblations.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jumbaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जुम्बक</span> <i>jumbaka</i> <a href="#entry-jumbaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> myth. np. de Jumbaka, forme mauvaise de <a href="#entry-Uvaru_na" class="skt-link">Varuṇa</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-juu">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">जू</span> <i>jū</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(javati)</i> pr. md. <i>(javate)</i> se dépêcher, être rapide.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-juur_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जूर्ण</span> <i>jūrṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-j_rr" class="skt-link">jṝ</a>] a. <a href="#entry-juur_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-juur_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-juur_naa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jūrṇā</i> vieux, gâté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jvaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्वाल</span> <i>jvāla</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jval" class="skt-link">jval</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jvaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jvaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jvaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jvālā</i> qui brûle, qui brille — <a href="#entry-jvaala" class="skt-link">m.</a> lampe; feu, flamme — <a href="#entry-jvaalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jvālā</i> flamme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jval">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्वल्</span> <i>jval</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(jvalati)</i> fut. <i>(</i><i>jvaliṣyati</i><i>)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>jvalita</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>ut</i>, <i>pra</i><i>)</i> brûler, briller — ca. <i>(jvalayati)</i> enflammer, allumer, illuminer — int. md. <i>(jājvalate)</i> briller intensément.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jvala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्वल</span> <i>jvala</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jval" class="skt-link">jval</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jvala" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jvala" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jvalaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jvalā</i> qui brûle, qui brille — <a href="#entry-jvala" class="skt-link">m.</a> flamme.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jvalana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्वलन</span> <i>jvalana</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-jval" class="skt-link">jval</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jvalana" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jvalana" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jvalanaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jvalanā</i> combustible, inflammable; en feu — <a href="#entry-jvalana" class="skt-link">m.</a> feu — <a href="#entry-jvalana" class="skt-link">n.</a> combustion; incendie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jvalita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्वलित</span> <i>jvalita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-jval" class="skt-link">jval</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jvalita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jvalita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jvalitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jvalitā</i> brillant.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jvar">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्वर्</span> <i>jvar</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-jval" class="skt-link">jval</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(jvarati)</i> avoir la fièvre.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jvara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्वर</span> <i>jvara</i> [act. <a href="#entry-jvar" class="skt-link">jvar</a>] <a href="#entry-jvara" class="skt-link">m.</a> fièvre; douleur.<br> <i>śrīnārāyaṇaḥ</i> <i>śītajvaram</i> <i>asṛjat</i> [BhP.] Le vénéré <a href="#entry-Unaaraaya_na" class="skt-link">Nārāyaṇa</a> envoya une fièvre froide (à <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>, pour combattre sa fièvre brûlante [<a href="#entry-dahat" class="skt-link">dahat</a>]).
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyaa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्या</span> <i>jyā_1</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-ji" class="skt-link">ji</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(jināti)</i> surpasser, opprimer; dompter.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyaa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्या</span> <i>jyā_2</i> [act. <a href="#entry-jyaa_1" class="skt-link">jyā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-jyaa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> oppression, suppression.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyaa_3">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्या</span> <i>jyā_3</i> <a href="#entry-jyaa_3" class="skt-link">f.</a> corde d'arc <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. [<a href="#entry-Uaaryabha_ta" class="skt-link">Āryabhaṭa</a>] sinus d'un angle; syn. <a href="#entry-jiiva" class="skt-link">jīva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyaaghaata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyāghāta</i> [<a href="#entry-ghaata" class="skt-link">ghāta</a>] <a href="#entry-jyaaghaata" class="skt-link">m.</a> blessure de rebond de la corde d'arc.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyaaghaatarekhaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyāghātarekhā</i> [<a href="#entry-rekhaa" class="skt-link">rekhā</a>] <a href="#entry-jyaaghaatarekhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> sa marque sur le poignet gauche.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyaaghaatavaara_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyāghātavāraṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-vaara_na" class="skt-link">vāraṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-jyaaghaatavaara_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> gant de protection de l'archer.<br> <a href="#entry-jyaaghaata" class="skt-link">jyāghāta</a> <br> <a href="#entry-jyaa_3" class="skt-link">jyā_3</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyaayas">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्यायस्</span> <i>jyāyas</i> [compar. <a href="#entry-jyaa_2" class="skt-link">jyā_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jyaayas" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jyaayas" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jyaayasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jyāyasī</i> supérieur; plus grand, plus fort <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> aîné — <a href="#entry-jyaayasii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jyāyasī</i> sœur aînée.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyai_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्यैष्ठ</span> <i>jyaiṣṭha</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-jye_s_tha" class="skt-link">jyeṣṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-jyai_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> astr. nom du mois de <a href="#entry-Ujye_s_thaa" class="skt-link">Jyeṣṭhā</a> (mai-juin); cf. <a href="#entry-nirjalaikaadazii" class="skt-link">nirjalaikādaśī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyai_s_thaa_s_tamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyaiṣṭhāṣṭamī</i> [<a href="#entry-a_s_tamii" class="skt-link">aṣṭamī</a>] <a href="#entry-jyai_s_thaa_s_tamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> soc. 8⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ jour de la quinzaine claire du mois <a href="#entry-jyai_s_tha" class="skt-link">jyaiṣṭha</a>; on y célèbre la naissance de <a href="#entry-Upaarvatii" class="skt-link">Pārvatī</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-jyai_s_tha" class="skt-link">jyaiṣṭha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyauti_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्यौतिष</span> <i>jyautiṣa</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-jyotis" class="skt-link">jyotis</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jyauti_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jyauti_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jyauti_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jyautiṣā</i> qui étudie ou connaît l'astronomie [<a href="#entry-jyoti_sa" class="skt-link">jyotiṣa</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyautsna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्यौत्स्न</span> <i>jyautsna</i> [vr. <a href="#entry-jyotis" class="skt-link">jyotis</a><a href="#entry--na" class="skt-link">-na</a>] <a href="#entry-jyautsna" class="skt-link">m.</a> partie claire de la lune — <a href="#entry-jyautsnii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jyautsnī</i> lumière de pleine lune.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jye_s_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्येष्ठ</span> <i>jyeṣṭha</i> [super. <a href="#entry-jyaa_2" class="skt-link">jyā_2</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jye_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jye_s_tha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jye_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jyeṣṭhā</i> le meilleur, le plus fort; prééminent; aîné — ifc. le meilleur en — <a href="#entry-jye_s_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> frère aîné <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> math. racine carrée — <a href="#entry-jye_s_thaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jyeṣṭhā</i> épouse aînée <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> (doigt) le majeur <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. de Jyeṣṭhā «Aînée (de <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a>)», déesse de mauvais augure née du barattage de la mer de lait [<a href="#entry-k_siirodamathana" class="skt-link">kṣīrodamathana</a>] avant <a href="#entry-Ulak_smii" class="skt-link">Lakṣmī</a>; les dieux l'envoyèrent en exil dans le ciel; aussi appelée Jyeṣṭhaghnī «Celle qui tue l'aîné» <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> astr. np. de <a href="#entry-Ujye_s_thaa" class="skt-link">Jyeṣṭhā</a>, 16⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ astérisme lunaire [<a href="#entry-nak_satra" class="skt-link">nakṣatra</a>], corr. à Antarès; <a href="#entry-Uindra" class="skt-link">Indra</a> y préside; son symbole est un anneau; elle personnifie la variole.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jye_s_thadeva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyeṣṭhadeva</i> [<a href="#entry-deva" class="skt-link">deva</a>] <a href="#entry-jye_s_thadeva" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. du mathématicien Jyeṣṭhadeva (1500–1610) du <a href="#entry-Ukerala" class="skt-link">Kerala</a>; on lui doit le traité d'analyse <a href="#entry-Uga_nitayuktibhaa_saa" class="skt-link">Gaṇitayuktibhāṣā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jye_s_thaghnii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyeṣṭhaghnī</i> [<a href="#entry-ghnii" class="skt-link">ghnī</a>] <a href="#entry-jye_s_thaghnii" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-jye_s_thaa" class="skt-link">jyeṣṭhā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jye_s_thapuur_nimaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyeṣṭhapūrṇimā</i> [<a href="#entry-puur_nimaa" class="skt-link">pūrṇimā</a>] <a href="#entry-jye_s_thapuur_nimaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> astr. pleine lune du mois de <a href="#entry-Ujye_s_thaa" class="skt-link">Jyeṣṭhā</a> (début juin); elle signale la fin de la saison des mangues, et le début de la mousson; cf. <a href="#entry-snaanapuur_nimaa" class="skt-link">snānapūrṇimā</a>, <a href="#entry-va_tapuur_nimaa" class="skt-link">vaṭapūrṇimā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jye_s_thya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyeṣṭhya</i> [<a href="#entry--ya" class="skt-link">-ya</a>] <a href="#entry-jye_s_thya" class="skt-link">n.</a> aînesse.<br> <a href="#entry-jye_s_tha" class="skt-link">jyeṣṭha</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyeya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्येय</span> <i>jyeya</i> [pfp. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <a href="#entry-jyaa_1" class="skt-link">jyā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jyeya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jyeya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jyeyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jyeyā</i> que l'on peut ou doit opprimer; dont on peut confisquer les biens.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyoti_h">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्योतिः</span> <i>jyotiḥ</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-jyotis" class="skt-link">jyotis</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyoti_h_s_tambha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyotiḥṣṭambha</i> [<a href="#entry-stambha" class="skt-link">stambha</a>] <a href="#entry-jyoti_h_s_tambha" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. mât couronné pour la fête de <a href="#entry-Ukaarttikeya" class="skt-link">Kārttikeya</a> d'un pot à feu enflammé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>jyotiṣṭambha</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyoti_h_s_toma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyotiḥṣṭoma</i> [<a href="#entry-stoma" class="skt-link">stoma</a>] <a href="#entry-jyoti_h_s_toma" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. véd. cérémonie de sacrifice de <a href="#entry-soma" class="skt-link">soma</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>jyotiṣṭoma</i> id.<br> <a href="#entry-jyoti_h" class="skt-link">jyotiḥ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyoti_hzaastra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyotiḥśāstra</i> [<a href="#entry-zaastra" class="skt-link">śāstra</a>] <a href="#entry-jyoti_hzaastra" class="skt-link">n.</a> astronomie, astrologie; syn. <a href="#entry-jyotirvidyaa" class="skt-link">jyotirvidyā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyoti_s">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्योतिष्</span> <i>jyotiṣ</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-jyotis" class="skt-link">jyotis</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyoti_sa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्योतिष</span> <i>jyotiṣa</i> [<a href="#entry-jyotis" class="skt-link">jyotis</a>] <a href="#entry-jyoti_sa" class="skt-link">m.</a> astronome <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> soc. magie; not. formule magique pour exorciser les armes — <a href="#entry-jyoti_sa" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. l'astronomie/astrologie, un des 6 traités annexes du <a href="#entry-Uveda" class="skt-link">Veda</a> [<a href="#entry-vedaafga" class="skt-link">vedāṅga</a>].
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyoti_savedaafga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyotiṣavedāṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-vedaafga" class="skt-link">vedāṅga</a>] <a href="#entry-jyoti_savedaafga" class="skt-link">n.</a> astr. np. du Jyotiṣavedāṅga, bref ouvrage dont on a 2 recensions: une courte de 36 versets associée au <a href="#entry-U_rgveda" class="skt-link">Ṛgveda</a>, composée par <a href="#entry-Ulagadha" class="skt-link">Lagadha</a>, et une longue plus récente de 43 versets associée au <a href="#entry-Uyajurveda" class="skt-link">Yajurveda</a>; aussi appelé <a href="#entry-Uvedaafgajyoti_sa" class="skt-link">Vedāṅgajyotiṣa</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-jyoti_sa" class="skt-link">jyotiṣa</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyoti_ska_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyotiṣkaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-ka_na" class="skt-link">kaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-jyoti_ska_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> étincelle.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyoti_smat">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyotiṣmat</i> [<a href="#entry--mat" class="skt-link">-mat</a>] <a href="#entry-jyoti_smat" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [ChU.] le Radiant ou partition [<a href="#entry-paada" class="skt-link">pāda</a>] du <a href="#entry-brahman" class="skt-link">brahman</a> en quatre radiances: le Feu [<a href="#entry-Uagni" class="skt-link">Agni</a>], le Soleil [<a href="#entry-Usuurya" class="skt-link">Sūrya</a>], la Lune [<a href="#entry-Ucandra" class="skt-link">Candra</a>] et l'Éclair [<a href="#entry-Uvidyut" class="skt-link">Vidyut</a>].<br> <a href="#entry-jyoti_s" class="skt-link">jyotiṣ</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyotir">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्योतिर्</span> <i>jyotir</i> iic. de <a href="#entry-jyotis" class="skt-link">jyotis</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyotirlifga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyotirliṅga</i> [<a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a>] <a href="#entry-jyotirlifga" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. «<a href="#entry-lifga" class="skt-link">liṅga</a> céleste»; syn. <a href="#entry-svayambhuulifga" class="skt-link">svayaṃbhūliṅga</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> [<i>dvādaśajyotirliṅga</i>] l'un des 12 signes radieux de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>: <a href="#entry-Uofkaarezvara" class="skt-link">Oṃkāreśvara</a>, <a href="#entry-Ukedaaranaatha" class="skt-link">Kedāranātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Ugh_r_s_nezvara" class="skt-link">Ghṛṣṇeśvara</a>, <a href="#entry-Utryambakezvara" class="skt-link">Tryambakeśvara</a>, <a href="#entry-Unaagezvara" class="skt-link">Nāgeśvara</a>, <a href="#entry-Ubhiimazafkara" class="skt-link">Bhīmaśaṅkara</a>, <a href="#entry-Umallikaarjuna" class="skt-link">Mallikārjuna</a>, <a href="#entry-Umahaakaalezvara" class="skt-link">Mahākāleśvara</a>, <a href="#entry-Uraamalifgezvara" class="skt-link">Rāmaliṅgeśvara</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvizvanaatha" class="skt-link">Viśvanātha</a>, <a href="#entry-Uvaidyanaatha" class="skt-link">Vaidyanātha</a>, et <a href="#entry-Usomanaatha" class="skt-link">Somanātha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyotirma_tha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyotirmaṭha</i> [<a href="#entry-ma_tha" class="skt-link">maṭha</a>] <a href="#entry-jyotirma_tha" class="skt-link">m.</a> monastère de l'<a href="#entry-uttaraamnaaya" class="skt-link">uttarāmnāya</a>, situé à <a href="#entry-Ubadariinaatha" class="skt-link">Badarīnātha</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyotirmaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyotirmaya</i> [<a href="#entry-maya" class="skt-link">maya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-jyotirmaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-jyotirmaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-jyotirmayii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jyotirmayī</i> fait de lumière, brillant; plein d'étoiles.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyotirvidyaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jyotirvidyā</i> [<a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a>] <a href="#entry-jyotirvidyaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> astronomie, astrologie; syn. <a href="#entry-jyoti_hzaastra" class="skt-link">jyotiḥśāstra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-jyotir" class="skt-link">jyotir</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyotis">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्योतिस्</span> <i>jyotis</i> [<a href="#entry-jyut" class="skt-link">jyut</a>] <a href="#entry-jyotis" class="skt-link">n.</a> clarté, lueur, lumière; radiance du ciel clair; éclair <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> étoile, corps céleste, clair de lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. pouvoir d'illumination; Jyotis est le Saint Esprit, ou lumière spirituelle divine exprimant le Divin en tant que synthèse d'Harmonie, Sagesse et Lumière; <a href="#entry-jyotis" class="skt-link">jyotis</a> abolit la distance entre la connaissance et l'action et apporte non seulement <a href="#entry-sukha" class="skt-link">sukha</a> le bonheur mental mais aussi <a href="#entry-aananda" class="skt-link">ānanda</a> la joie et la félicité divines <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pl. <i>jyotīṃṣi</i> les astres <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> hi. <i>Joshi</i>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyotsnaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्योत्स्ना</span> <i>jyotsnā</i> [<a href="#entry-jyotis" class="skt-link">jyotis</a>] <a href="#entry-jyotsnaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> clair de lune <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>jyotsnī</i> id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-jyut">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्युत्</span> <i>jyut</i> [relié à <a href="#entry-dyut_1" class="skt-link">dyut_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] pr. <i>(jyotati)</i> pr. md. <i>(jyotate)</i> luire, briller — ca. <i>(jyotayati)</i> illuminer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञ</span> <i>jña</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-j_naa_1" class="skt-link">jñā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-j_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-j_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naa_2" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jñā_2</i> ifc. connaisseur; intelligent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> qui sait, qui connaît; qui reconnaît; intelligent <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> familier de; versé dans — <a href="#entry-j_na" class="skt-link">m.</a> sage <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> phil. [<a href="#entry-safkhya" class="skt-link">saṃkhya</a>] l'Esprit pensant <a href="#entry-Upuru_sa" class="skt-link">Puruṣa</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> myth. np. Jña épith. de <a href="#entry-Ubudha" class="skt-link">Budha</a> «Qui a le savoir».
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naa_1">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    √ <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञा</span> <i>jñā_1</i> v. [<span class="skt-grammar">9</span>] pr. <i>(jānāti)</i> pr. md. <i>(jānīte)</i> pft. <i>(jajñau)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">1</span>] <i>(ajñāt)</i> aor. ps. <i>(ajñāyi)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>jñāta</i><i>)</i> abs. <i>(</i><i>jñātvā</i>, <i>-jñāya</i><i>)</i> pf. <i>(</i><i>anu</i>, <i>abhi</i>, <i>ava</i>, <i>ā</i>, <i>pari</i>, <i>pra</i>, <i>prati</i>, <i>vi</i>, <i>sam</i><i>)</i> connaître, savoir, prendre connaissance de; apprendre; connaître comme ⟨2 acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> se rappeler de ⟨g.⟩; engager un rite avec ⟨g.⟩; imaginer, supposer ⟨g.⟩ — ps. <i>(jñāyate)</i> être informé de — ca. <i>(jñāpayati)</i> ca. <i>(jñapayati)</i> faire savoir, informer — ca. md. <i>(jñāpayate)</i> ca. md. <i>(jñapayate)</i> s'informer — dés. md. <i>(jijñāsate)</i> désirer savoir <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> lat. <i>gnosco</i>; ang. to know; fr. connaître.<br> <i>jñāyatām</i> adr. (ps. imp.) renseigne-toi, cherche.<br> <i>sukhasya</i> <i>jānāti</i> il se rappelle du bonheur.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naa_2">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञा</span> <i>jñā_2</i> cf. <a href="#entry-j_na" class="skt-link">jña</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naana">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञान</span> <i>jñāna</i> [act. <a href="#entry-j_naa_1" class="skt-link">jñā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naana" class="skt-link">n.</a> connaissance, savoir, science; sagesse; syn. <a href="#entry-vidyaa" class="skt-link">vidyā</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> perspicacité; intelligence, pensée; conscience; syn. <a href="#entry-buddhi" class="skt-link">buddhi</a> <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> fr. gnose, cognition.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanaakaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānākāra</i> [<a href="#entry-aakaara_1" class="skt-link">ākāra_1</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanaakaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-navyanyaaya" class="skt-link">navyanyāya</a>] structure cognitive.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanaatma">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānātma</i> [<a href="#entry-aatma" class="skt-link">ātma</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanaatma" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. conscient, qui a gardé sa connaissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanabhuumikaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānabhūmikā</i> [<a href="#entry-bhuumikaa" class="skt-link">bhūmikā</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanabhuumikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> phil. niveau de connaissance; cf. <a href="#entry-saptaj_naanabhuumikaa" class="skt-link">saptajñānabhūmikā</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanadiik_saa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānadīkṣā</i> [<a href="#entry-diik_saa" class="skt-link">dīkṣā</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanadiik_saa" class="skt-link">f.</a> du. <i>jñānadīkṣe</i> la connaissance et le rite.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanajaala">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānajāla</i> [<a href="#entry-jaala" class="skt-link">jāla</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanajaala" class="skt-link">n.</a> mod. réseau sémantique; ontologie.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanakarmasamuccaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānakarmasamuccaya</i> [<a href="#entry-karma" class="skt-link">karma</a>-<a href="#entry-samuccaya" class="skt-link">samuccaya</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanakarmasamuccaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. combinaison de la connaissance et les actes.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanakarmasamuccayavaada">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānakarmasamuccayavāda</i> [<a href="#entry-vaada" class="skt-link">vāda</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanakarmasamuccayavaada" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. doctrine philosophique qui postule que les actes doivent complémenter la connaissance pour obtenir la libération; elle fut défendue par <a href="#entry-Ubhart_rprapa_nca" class="skt-link">Bhartṛprapañca</a> et réfutée par <a href="#entry-Uzafkara_1" class="skt-link">Śaṃkara_1</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-j_naanakarmasamuccaya" class="skt-link">jñānakarmasamuccaya</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanakha_dga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānakhaḍga</i> [<a href="#entry-kha_dga" class="skt-link">khaḍga</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanakha_dga" class="skt-link">m.</a> bd. «glaive de sagesse»; brandi par l'accompli [<a href="#entry-bodhisattva" class="skt-link">bodhisattva</a>] <a href="#entry-Uma_njuzrii" class="skt-link">Mañjuśrī</a>, il tranche l'ignorance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanakha_n_da">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānakhaṇḍa</i> [<a href="#entry-kha_n_da" class="skt-link">khaṇḍa</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanakha_n_da" class="skt-link">m.</a> lit. np. du Jñānakhaṇḍa «Chapitre de la connaissance», partie du SP. traitant de la connaissance de la vérité suprême.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanalak_sa_na">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānalakṣaṇa</i> [<a href="#entry-lak_sa_na" class="skt-link">lakṣaṇa</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanalak_sa_na" class="skt-link">n.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-nyaaya" class="skt-link">nyāya</a>] perception spécifique faisant appel aux connaissances, not. au sens commun ou à la mémoire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanamaarga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānamārga</i> [<a href="#entry-maarga" class="skt-link">mārga</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanamaarga" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. voie de la connaissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naananetra">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānanetra</i> [<a href="#entry-netra" class="skt-link">netra</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naananetra" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] [«œil de savoir»] 3⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ œil, ouvert à l'<a href="#entry-aaj_naacakra" class="skt-link">ājñācakra</a>; il contemple la Vérité Divine; c'est un attribut de <a href="#entry-Uziva" class="skt-link">Śiva</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanapa_t_ta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānapaṭṭa</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_t_ta" class="skt-link">paṭṭa</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanapa_t_ta" class="skt-link">m.</a> jeu de société, métaphore de la rétribution des bonnes et mauvaises actions; il se joue sur un damier de 72 (<a href="#entry-vai_s_nava" class="skt-link">vaiṣṇava</a>) ou 84 (<a href="#entry-jaina" class="skt-link">jaina</a>) cases.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanapa_ncamii">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānapañcamī</i> [<a href="#entry-pa_ncamii" class="skt-link">pañcamī</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanapa_ncamii" class="skt-link">f.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-zrutapa_ncamii" class="skt-link">śrutapañcamī</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanasampanna">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānasampanna</i> [<a href="#entry-sampanna" class="skt-link">sampanna</a>] a. <a href="#entry-j_naanasampanna" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naanasampanna" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naanasampannaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jñānasampannā</i> doué de sagesse.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanav_rddha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānavṛddha</i> [<a href="#entry-v_rddha" class="skt-link">vṛddha</a>] a. <a href="#entry-j_naanav_rddha" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naanav_rddha" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-vayov_rddhaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>vayovṛddhā</i> avancé en connaissances.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanayoga">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānayoga</i> [<a href="#entry-yoga" class="skt-link">yoga</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanayoga" class="skt-link">m.</a> phil. réalisation spirituelle par la voie de la connaissance; cf. <a href="#entry-saayujya" class="skt-link">sāyujya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanendriya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānendriya</i> [<a href="#entry-indriya" class="skt-link">indriya</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanendriya" class="skt-link">m.</a> cf. <a href="#entry-buddhiindriya" class="skt-link">buddhīndriya</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanezvaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñāneśvāra</i> [<a href="#entry-iizvara" class="skt-link">īśvara</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naanezvaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> hist. np. de Jnāneśvāra <a href="#entry-Umahaaraaja" class="skt-link">Mahārāja</a>, saint et poète marathe du 13⟨sup⟩e⟨/sup⟩ siècle; il écrivit not. la Jnāneśvārī, un commentaire en vers de la <a href="#entry-Ubhagavadgiitaa" class="skt-link">Bhagavadgītā</a>, à l'âge de 16 ans.<br> <a href="#entry-j_naana" class="skt-link">jñāna</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naanin">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñānin</i> [<a href="#entry-j_naana" class="skt-link">jñāna</a><a href="#entry--in" class="skt-link">-in</a>] a. <a href="#entry-j_naanin" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naanin" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naaninii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jñāninī</i> éclairé, perspicace; qui sait, qui comprend, qui possède la connaissance.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञाप्</span> <i>jñāp</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-j_naa_1" class="skt-link">jñā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(jñāpayati)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>jñāpita</i><i>)</i> faire savoir, informer — pr. md. <i>(jñāpayate)</i> s'informer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naapaka">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञापक</span> <i>jñāpaka</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-j_naap" class="skt-link">jñāp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-j_naapaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naapaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naapikaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jñāpikā</i> informateur, qui renseigne — <a href="#entry-j_naapaka" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. maître de requêtes (dignitaire de cour royale) — <a href="#entry-j_naapaka" class="skt-link">n.</a> indice révélateur; instrument cognitif; précepte <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> gram. indice révélateur de l'existence d'une règle métalinguistique [<a href="#entry-paribhaa_saa" class="skt-link">paribhāṣā</a>] sans laquelle une règle serait superflue.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naapita">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञापित</span> <i>jñāpita</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-j_naap" class="skt-link">jñāp</a>] a. <a href="#entry-j_naapita" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naapita" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naapitaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jñāpitā</i> informé; instruit en ⟨acc.⟩ <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> rendu public; enseigné — ifc. connu par.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naat_r">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञातृ</span> <i>jñātṛ</i> [agt. <a href="#entry-j_naa_1" class="skt-link">jñā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-j_naat_r" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naat_r" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naatrii" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jñātrī</i> phil. le sujet connaissant; opp. <a href="#entry-pramaat_r" class="skt-link">pramātṛ</a>.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naata">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञात</span> <i>jñāta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-j_naa_1" class="skt-link">jñā_1</a>] a. <a href="#entry-j_naata" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naata" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naataa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jñātā</i> connu; appris; perçu, compris <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> pris comme ⟨nom.⟩; compris comme ⟨nom.⟩ (par ⟨g.⟩) <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> intentionnel.<br> <i>jñātam</i> (impers.) c'est connu; on sait.<br> <i>mayā</i> <i>jñātam</i> je voulais dire.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naatasambandha">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñātasambandha</i> [<a href="#entry-sambandha" class="skt-link">sambandha</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naatasambandha" class="skt-link">m.</a> parenté bien connue, relation évidente.<br> <a href="#entry-j_naata" class="skt-link">jñāta</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naati">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञाति</span> <i>jñāti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-j_naa_1" class="skt-link">jñā_1</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naati" class="skt-link">m.</a> proche; parent.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naatibhaava">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñātibhāva</i> [<a href="#entry-bhaava" class="skt-link">bhāva</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naatibhaava" class="skt-link">m.</a> soc. lien de parenté.<br> <a href="#entry-j_naati" class="skt-link">jñāti</a>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naatipraaya">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñātiprāya</i> [<a href="#entry-praaya" class="skt-link">prāya</a>] a. <a href="#entry-j_naatipraaya" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naatipraaya" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naatipraayaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jñātiprāyā</i> principalement destiné à la famille.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naatitva">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñātitva</i> [<a href="#entry--tva" class="skt-link">-tva</a>] <a href="#entry-j_naatitva" class="skt-link">m.</a> consanguinité; relation de parenté.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_naatvaa">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञात्वा</span> <i>jñātvā</i> [abs. <a href="#entry-j_naa_1" class="skt-link">jñā_1</a>] ind. sachant, ayant pris connaissance de.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_nap">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञप्</span> <i>jñap</i> [ca. <a href="#entry-j_naa_1" class="skt-link">jñā_1</a>] v. [<span class="skt-grammar">10</span>] pr. <i>(jñapayati)</i> aor. [<span class="skt-grammar">3</span>] <i>(ajijñapat)</i> pp. <i>(</i><i>jñapta</i>, <i>jñapita</i><i>)</i> faire savoir, informer — pr. md. <i>(jñapayate)</i> s'informer.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_napta">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञप्त</span> <i>jñapta</i> [pp. <a href="#entry-j_nap" class="skt-link">jñap</a>] a. <a href="#entry-j_napta" class="skt-link">m.</a> <a href="#entry-j_napta" class="skt-link">n.</a> <a href="#entry-j_naptaa" class="skt-link">f.</a> <i>jñaptā</i> instruit; informé <span class="skt-pipe">|</span> var. <i>jñapita</i> f. id.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_napti">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञप्ति</span> <i>jñapti</i> [act. <a href="#entry-j_nap" class="skt-link">jñap</a>] <a href="#entry-j_napti" class="skt-link">f.</a> compréhension.
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-j_navaara">
  <div class="skt-entry-content">
    <i>jñavāra</i> [<a href="#entry-vaara" class="skt-link">vāra</a>] <a href="#entry-j_navaara" class="skt-link">m.</a> mercredi (jour de Mercure); syn. <a href="#entry-budhavaara" class="skt-link">budhavāra</a>.<br> <a href="#entry-j_na" class="skt-link">jña</a>
  </div>
</div>
